1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/sid+d+ha e ka bI ra ma hA tan+t+ra rA dza nA ma/bod skad du/dpa' bo gcig pu grub pa zhes bya ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po/bcom ldan 'das 
1b2(rgyud): 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la phyag 'tshal lo//dpa' bo gcig grub dpa' bo'i gtso//rab mchog smra ba rnams kyi mchog/'gro ba'i bla ma 'jam rdo rjes//'jig rten don du 
1b3(rgyud): rgyud rab gsungs//lha nyid gsang sngags tshul gyis na//dngos grub thams cad stsol mdzad pa//sngags pas de nyid bsgoms nas ni//de grub na ni 'grub 'gyur te//sngags 
1b4(rgyud): pa lus ni rnam dag dang //gang tshe dngos grub phra mo 'grub//snod du gyur pa'i lus la ni//dngos grub chen po rab tu skye//rim pa 'di yis 'di la ni//sa la sogs pa 'thob pa 
1b5(rgyud): skye//sngags pas yid bzhin nor bu ltar//rtag tu sems can don byed do//de ltar mkhyen nas thugs brtses non//rdo rje 'chang sogs kyis zhus tshe//thub pa'i gsang 
2a1(rgyud): @#//sngags bsdus pa ni//'gro ba'i don du gsungs pa yin//oM kA lu me lu kA lu me stam+b+ha la sid+d+ha lA pa raM hA ra ra nyu lu n+tu e khu ts+tsha i swA hA/gsang sngags kyi rgyal po 'di ni legs par bzlas na ser ba gcod par byed do//dran pa nyid dang bral ba la 
2a2(rgyud): mngon par bzlas pas ser ba 'bab pa'am khab 'bab pa gcod par 'gyur zhing gang du 'dod pa der 'gro'o//de bzhin du zlog dang chung bar rdo rje dang thog 'bab pa la sogs pa med par byed do//oM ga ru Da haM sa te/tsa la tsa la swA hA/sngags kyi rgyal 
2a3(rgyud): po 'di dran pa nyid ni ser ba 'bab pa zlog par byed do//rdo rgyus kyis rnga ra Ta ha la bris shing /rnga lcag la mngon par bzlas pa'i rnga pa Ta ha'i sgras be rab 'bab pa zlog par byed//de bzhin du dung la sogs pa'i sgras kyang bya'o//oM he te/tsin+ta ba tiSh+Ta/ban+d+ha 
2a4(rgyud): ban+d+ha/d+hA ra la d+hA ra ya/nai run+ti ne run+ta/che ge mo Ur+N+Na ma Ni swA hA/gsang sngagasakyi rgyal po 'di sngon du bsnyen pa'i tshogs 'bum phrag gcig yongs su bzlas na 'grub par 'gyur ro//zlog dang chung dang rdo rje dang thog 'bab pa'am rab la sogs pa dran pa tsam 
2a5(rgyud): nyid kyis zlog par byed do//thal ba dang yungs ka ra la mngon par bzlas na/char ches pa gcod pa dang byi ba dang skrangs la sogs pa'i mchu 'ching bar byed do//skyed mos tshal dang zhing la byad rab bu srin dang byi ba rnams kyis nye bar tshe bzlog par byed do//dur 
2a6(rgyud): khrod kyi ras la ming dang bcas pa'i sngags bris la sgra tshil khyis brtsams te/dur khrod kyi bum par bcug nas khar cad de/de nyid du sbas pas mngal rengs par 'gyur ro//byung nas 'o ma dang chus bkrus te chu la bor bas zhi bar 'gyur ro//yung ba'am/
2b1(rgyud): bzlas grog ngam gos sam lam la mngon par bris te/sgra tshal gyi nang du gtums pa'i sngags 'di grog mkhar gyis las 'jigs byed kyi gzugs su byas pa de'i snying gar bcug ste de'i bcas bsal kar nus sbas na/rgol ba dang phyir rgol ba'i kha rengs par byed do//rtsod 
2b2(rgyud): spyod byed pa na gzugs de nyid kyi lce la lan bdun mngon par bsngags pa'i tsher ma bdun gyis btsugs na kha 'ching bar byed par 'gyur ro//rdo rgyus kyis kham phor la bris te thal bas bkang la/kha sbyar te sbas na mi snyan pa brjod pa kha rengs par byed do//rnga mkhan gyi lag pa 
2b3(rgyud): phyis pa'i 'jim pa las/lug gi gzugs ma byas pa'i snying gar sgra tshil gyis gtums pa'i sngags bcug na khros pa'i kha rengs par byed do/ke'am dpung pa la sngags btags na g.yul rogs kyi mtshon cha'i dpal zhi bar byed do//grog mkhar gyi 'jim pa las 
2b4(rgyud): byas pa'i tshogs kyi bdag po'i rkang pa'i mthil du sngags kyi rgyal po 'di bcug ste/bzhi mdor sbas na 'gro ba dang 'ong ba thams cad rengs par byed cing rtag par byed pa thams cad rengs par byed//thog 'am gos la sngags kyi rgyal po 'di bris la sgra tshal gyis gtums te de nyid 
2b5(rgyud): kyi khong bar bcug la/sum pa sa rab chus bkang ba'i nang du bcug na yongs su brtag pa rengs par byed do//bla gos kyi mtha' ma la sngags rjes su dran zhing mdud pa bcings pas lam du rkun po la sogs pa rengs par byed cing /nags kyi ba men la sogs pa rwa can 
2b6(rgyud): dang mche ba can rnams rengs par byed do//khong pa la lan bdun sngags te chur bcug na/chun rgyu ba li ba 'ching bar byed do//dur khrod kyi ram la pha rol gyi dpung gi tshogs kyi skyes bu'i gtso bo'i ming smos pa'i sngags sna tshogs rdo rje'i dbus su bris la/sna tshogs rdo rje'i phyi rol 
2b7(rgyud): du mi ge laM brgyad bris shing phyi rol dbang chen rmi dkyil 'khor dang ldan par bris te/phyogs rdo rjes brgyan pa'i sgra tshil las byas pa'i tshogs kyi bdag po'i bong bar bcug pa'i sngags/pha rol gyi dpung gi tshogasakyis mtshams kyi bar du bcug na dpung gi tshogs rengs par byed do//
3a1(rgyud): @#//sngags oM gnyis kyi kha sbyor du bcug la yi ge tha zung gis yongs su bskor ba/phyi rol du yi ge laM brgyad kyis bskor ba dbang chen gyi dkyil 'khor gyis non pa sna tshogs rdo rjes nye bar mdzes par byas pa/grog mkhar gyi 'jim pa las byas pa'i tshogs kyi 
3a2(rgyud): bdag po'i khong bar bcug la/dur khrod kyi bum pa'i nang du bcug nas dur khrod du sbas pa'am/phub mo'i sbyin sreg gis pha rol gyi dpung gi tshogs rengs par byed do//grong khyer tshig pa'i me la mngon du phyogs shing sngags 'dis mngon par bsngags pa'i chu snyim pa bdun rab tu gtor 
3a3(rgyud): na khyim gang bsrung bar 'dod pa'i khyim de srung bar byed do//thal bas mgul ba bcings pas skyugs pa zlog par byed do//mthe'u chung gi rtse dam du btsir la sngags brjod pas skyigs bu med par byed do//thal ba la mngon par bsngags pas mig rengs pa sos par byed 
3a4(rgyud): do//'brum bu la sogs pa rnam par 'tshe ba la ras kyi dum bu la bres te/khyim gang gi sgor btags pa'i khyim der 'brum bu la sogs pa rnams par 'tshe ba zlog par byed do//nya dang sha dang chang dang 'bras chan la sogs pa sbyin par 'gyur ba las grong gi phyi rol gyi char gtor 
3a5(rgyud): ma chen po gtang bar bya ba dang lam shing lnga dang 'bru lnga'i bye brag mar la btags pas grong gi dbus su zhi ba'i thab khung byas la sbyin sreg byas na bdud dang rnam par 'tshe ba thams cad rengs par byed do//gang 'ga' zhig la grong la sogs pa 'byin par 'gyur ba la bzlas pa tsam 
3a6(rgyud): gyis zlog par byed do//rtag tu bzlas na lha dang lha ma yin dang lto 'phye chen po rnams kyis mi mthong bar 'gyur ro//dpal rje btsun sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mngon par byang chub pa'i dus su/bdud bzhi las rgyal bar bya ba'i don du sngags kyi rgyal po 'di mdzod spu 
3a7(rgyud): las rnam par phyung ba'i phyir mdzod spu'i nor bu zhes bya bar grags pa yin no//gseg ma la mngon par bsngags te gtor bas sbrul dga' phyer bar byed do//khyim gyi rtsig ngos la rdo rgyus la mngon par bsngags pas bris na sbrul gyis zil gyis non par mi 'gyur ro//yang mdo 
3b1(rgyud): li ba la sngags gzong bus brkos te bris pa sbas na ri dgas dang skal gdug pa rnams khyim mam grong ngam grong khyer du sbas na ci srid 'dod pa'i bar du mche ba 'ching bar byed do//rgyud pas grol bar 'gyur ro//sngags 'di ma bsgrubs su zin kyang las thams cad byed par 'gyur ro//gseg 
3b2(rgyud): ma la sogs pa la yongs su bzlas te gtor na mche ba can thams cad kyi mche ba 'ching bar byed de//dbang chen gyi dkyil 'khor gyis khyab pa'i sna tshogs rdo rje'i dbus su bsgrub par bya ba'i ming dang bcas pa'i sngags thams cad bri bar bya'o//dur khrod kyi rdo bsregs pa'i shing 
3b3(rgyud): gi phur bu la lan bdun mngon par bsngags pa sgo'i steng du sbas na/skyed kyis 'tsho ba thams cad kyi skyed med par byed do//phyung bas grol bar 'gyur ro//oM ni hi ni tsA la a b+ha ya b+ha yaM ka ri/e lu pe lu tsu le lu/shi lA ya Da i dza ha le/pa T+Ta i dzaT+Ta/e pe lu A ga sa 
3b4(rgyud): pa the ke ra te/i a tu mud+d+hA ri ma d+ha ri bi nI bi lA mo ha ni thi b+hi da kA raM dza hi ShA kA ra Ni hi Ni Sha maHswA hA/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di sngar ma smos pa'i las thams cad ma bsgrubs su zin kyang byed par 'gyur ro//de las gzhan yang mngon par 'dod pa'i las de dag thams cad byed par 
3b5(rgyud): the tshom med do//lan tshwa dang ske cho mnyam pa'i sbyin sreg gam phub ma'i sbyin sreg gis pha rol gyi dpung gi tshogs rnams 'byer bar byed do//oM amA sim+na ka s+yA su dzo me phe Da i du s+yA su swA hA/zhes byung ngo //oM am pA shim b+hA kap+pa su dzo me bi du swA su/sngags kyi rgyal 
3b6(rgyud): po 'di ma bsgrubs su zin kyang rtag tu bzlas na 'jigs pa thams cad zlog par byed do//rdo rje'i chu'i thig les dgra bo thams cad rmongs par byed cing khros pas rab tu dang bar byed do//bcings pa la bzlas na bcings pa grol bar byed do//phongs par gyur pas bzlas na rnyed pa 'grub par 
3b7(rgyud): 'gyur ro//e Sho na bi to/hu lu hu dzA lA dzi tA/e Sho dzI tI to hu lu hu lu/dzA lA dzi hA hu lu ru DA+e ba pra ti rA tsA tu hu lu swA hA/hu lu hu lu la tri bo t+thi to hu lu ta tri ba pra ti gats+tsha nU hu lu hu lu swA hA/ljags kyi rgyal po 'dis 'thab pa dang 'thab mo dang rnam par rtsod pa thams cad la bzlas pa 
4a1(rgyud): @#//byas pas zhi bar byed do//phub ma'i sbyin sreg gis kyang thams cad du zhi bar 'gyur ro//ji ltar rnyed pa'i me tog la sngags rjod de/chu la bskur na thams cad du zhi bar 'gyu zhing rnam par rgyal ba thob par the tshom med do//gro ba khyer tshig pa'i me la mngon du phyogs pas 
4a2(rgyud): 'dug ste/mngon par bsngags pa'i chu snyem pa bdun gtor na/khyim gang bsrung bar 'dod pa'i khyim de srung bar byed cing mes tshig pa'i me ge ba las thar bar byed do//rkang gnyis dang rkang bzhi'i mche ba la phub ma'i sbyin sreg gis zhi bar byed do//oM pad+me pad+ma k+Shi pad+ma su b+ha ge phuH/pad+me 
4a3(rgyud): pad+ma/ak+She pad+ma/su b+ha ge phuHswA hA/sngags 'di thal ba la bzlas te bskus pas mig rgya btab pa rengs pa sos par byed do//byams pas mig rengs pa sos par byed de//lan bdun mngon par bsngags pas mig gis khros pa mthong ma thag tu rab tu dad par byed do//
4a4(rgyud): lan bdun mngon par bsngag pa'i chus gdong bkrus na skye bo thams cad dga' bar byed do//rtsig pa la ming smos pa'i sngags rdo rgyus kyis bris na mig nad thams cad 'joms par byed do//dben par 'dug ste sems rtse gcig pas skyes bu'i mig gnyis la mngon par bsngags 
4a5(rgyud): pa byas na/gang gi tshig lon pa 'gul bar gyur pa de'i tshe/bsams pa'i dgos pa 'grub par ston pa yin no//g.yas pa 'gul bar byed na mi dge ba'i dgos pa ston par byed do//oM mo tsa ni mo tsa la/mo k+Sha ni mo k+Sha ya/dI paM ba ra de swA hA/'dis mngon par bsngags 
4a6(rgyud): pa'i til mar gyis skye ba'i sgo la bskus na/sbrum ma bde bar btsa' bar byed do//bcings pa la mngon du phyogs par byed pas/lan bdun mngon par bsngags pa'i chu snyim pa brtan mngon par gtor ba dus gsum du byas pas bcings pa rnams grol bar byed do//
4a7(rgyud): gur gum mam ge wang gis grog la mngon par bris te/bcings pa'i mgo bo la btags na bcings pa las grol bar byed do//rtag tu bzlas na bcings pa grol bar byed do//oM ha ri marka Ta nA ya/sa hA sra bA huH/de ba da ta taM/ban d+ha nan mo kA ya swA hA/rdo rgyus la 
4b1(rgyud): mngon par bsngags pas sa'i steng du yang dang yang du bris te/lugs las bzlog pas phyis na bcings pa las grol bar byed do//lo ma la ming dang sngags su bcas pa mngon par bris te/mgo bo la btags na bcings pa thams cad grol bar 'gyur ro//oM tA ri Ni tA ra ya/
4b2(rgyud): ma tsa ni mo tsa ya/rma k+Sha ni mo k+Sha ya/dzI baM ba ra de swA hA/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di legs par bzlas na las thams cad byed par 'gyur zhing /yan bdun mngon par bsngags pa'i til mar gyis skye ba'i sgo la bskus na sbrum ma bde bar btsa' bar 'gyur ro//lan bdun mngon 
4b3(rgyud): par bsngags pa'i chu 'thungs na/mngal 'khyim pa bde bar btsa' bar byed do//rtag tu bzlas pas bdag dang gzhan 'ching ba las thar bar 'gyur ro//ske 'am dpung pa la bsrung ba btags pas 'jigs pa thams cad las srung bar byed do//oM tA re tu t+tA re tu re mo k+Sha ya dzI baM swA 
4b4(rgyud): r+pa/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di sngar smos pa'i bya ba byed par 'gyur ro//gur gum gyis gro ga la gang gi ming smos pa'i sngags mngon par bris la spra tshil du gtums te/dri zhim po'i chus bkang ba'i bum pa gsar pa'i nang du bcug ste/me tog dri zhim po la sogs pas dus 
4b5(rgyud): gsum du mchod pa byas na 'thab pa dang 'thab mo dang rtsod pa thams cad las rnam par rgyal bar 'gyur ro//gur gum kyi gro ga la gang gis ming smos pa/oM e ra N+Da s+ya ba ne kA ko//rang gA tI ra mu ya s+thi taHyi ba tu rU ta/yA nI ya m+bi shalA b+ha ba tu gurbi NI/oM e ra NA ba ne kA gaHgang+gA tI ra 
4b6(rgyud): mu ba s+thi taHpi ba tu rU taHsA nI ya s+thi sha l+yA p+ta btu gurbi swA hA/lan bdun mngon par bsngags pa'i chu khyor ba gsum po nyer 'ongs pas 'thungs na/sbrum ma bde bar btsa' bar byed do//oM a ma ra Ni dz+wi ban ti ye swA hA/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di legs par bzlas na las thams cad byed par 'gyur 
4b7(rgyud): ro//mngon par bsngags pa'i chu 'thungs na 'brum bu 'ong bar mi 'gyur ro//lus las skyes pa'i dug mi bzad pa dang spre'u dug dang sbrul dug la sogs pa'i rma la yung ba dang a ru ra dang ru tal sogs pa la yongs su bzlas te bskus na rang bzhin du gnas par 'gyur ro//nad dang rnam par 'tshe ba 
5a1(rgyud): @#//thams cad la sngags mdud bcings na zhi bar byed do//rtag tu bzlas na tshe zad pa yang tshe lo brgya thub par 'gyur ro//mar dang til gyi sbyin sreg gis thams cad zhi bar byed do//mngon par bsngags pa'i sman gyi btungs pas nad thams cad las yongs su thar bar byed 
5a2(rgyud): do//lan brgya bkrus pa'i mar la mngon par bsngags pas na ba'i yan lag la bskus na rang bzhin du gnas par 'gyur ro//mgo'i nad la til mar la bsngags te bskus pas rang bzhin du gnas par gyur ro//gdug pa'i rmal chu la bsngags nas gtor pas sos bar 
5a3(rgyud): 'gyur ro//smri ha'i nad la be dang la sogs pa'i mtshon gyis gtubs na smri ha bcad par 'gyur ro//ni rgun rnyi dang nim pa'i rtsa ba rma bya'i mjug sgro dang bcas pa gcig tu sbyar la/brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par bsngags pa'i bdug pas bdugs na/rims nyin gcig 
5a4(rgyud): pa dang /nyin gnyis pa dang /nyin gsum pa dang /nyin bzhi pa la sogs pa sel bar byed cing /'byung po dang /sha za dang /tshangs pa dang /srin po la sogs pa sel bar 'gyur ro//bud med bu shi bas lan brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par bsdags pa'i bum pas khrus 
5a5(rgyud): byas na bug son po dang ldan par 'gyur ro//grog la bris pa'i sngags rked pa'am dpung pa la btags pas mngal srung bar 'gyur ro//sngags rjes su dran pas g.yul ngogs su 'jug na rgyal ba rnyed par 'gyur zhing byabs pas nad thams cad sel bar byed do//oM pA 
5a6(rgyud): da tsA la ne swA hA/sngags kyi rgyal po'i di sngon du bsnyen pa'i tshogs legs par bsgrubs na las thams cad byed do//phol mig gi 'jigs pa la mngon par bsngags pa'i chu 'thungs na phol mig med par 'gyur ro//grog 'am shog gul bris te sgo la btags pas phol 
5a7(rgyud): mig med par 'gyur ro/'di ni 'tshe ba thams cad la mar la btags pa'i til gyi sbyin sreg gis srung ba chen po yin no//kha'i sgro lag pas bzung ste bzlas pa khri byas la gang gi khang pa'i steng gi logs su sbas pas de 'byer bar byed do//oM bi shA tsi par+N+Na sha wa ris rmo pa dra 
5b1(rgyud): ba nA sha ni swA hA/rig pa chen mo 'di ma bsgrubs su zin kyang rkang gnyis pa dang rkang bzhi pa thams cad kyi rnam par 'tshe ba sel bar byed cing /las thams cad byed par 'gyur ro//sbyin sreg gam bzlas pa'am khrus sam sngags mdud dam mngon par bsngags pa'i chu 
5b2(rgyud): 'thungs pa'am byabs pas nad thams cad sel bar byed do//gro ga la gur gum gyis bris pa'i sngags ske 'am dpung pa la btags na rtsod sbyor la rgyal ba thob par 'gyur zhing /rims nyin bzhi pa la sogs pa mi bzad pa las rnam par grol bar 'gyur ro//srin po la

5b4(rgyud): a mu ka s+ya tsa k+Shu stam+b+ha ya ti thA waHswAhA/tshes gang la mig na bar gyur pa de la rtsig pa'am spang leb la rdo rgyus kyis tshes bris la yi ge e kha sbyar gsum du bya'o//oM tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha hUM phaT/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di lan cig bzlas pas sdig pa thams cad nges par 
5b5(rgyud): sreg par byed do//'byung po la sogs pa'i gdon gyis zin pa la yungs ka ra la sogs pas brdegs pas gdon thams cad 'byer bar byed do//kham phor la rdo rgyus kyis bris te sgo la btags pas gsar du btsas pa'i byis pa srung bar byed do//mA tshil gyis gzugs 
5b6(rgyud): sor bzhi pa byas pa de'i snying gar bsgrub bya'i ming dang bcas pa'i sngags mngon par bris te bcug la de'i kha la tshe dam pa tsugs na phyir rgol ba'i kha phur bus btab par 'gyur ro//rkang pa la btsugs na 'gro ba dang 'ong ba'i nges par byed do//snying ga la btsugs na khro ba 
5b7(rgyud): 'joms par byed do//mi'i rje ngar gyi rus pa'i phur bu'am/lcags kyi phur bus gdab par bya'o//ming nas bzung zhing yan lag gang dang gang la phur bus gdab pa de dang de 'jig par 'gyur ro//dgra bo'i sgo'i 'og tu sbas na 'byer bar byed do//dur khrod kyi thal ba la 
6a1(rgyud): @#//mngon par bsngags pas sgo'i ya them la gtor na 'byer ro//ral gri la mngon par bsngags la g.yul du 'dzug na rgyal ba sgrub par byed do//bya ba gang la 'jug pa na gtor ma nye bar bstabs na de dang de 'grub par 'gyur ro//sngags pa 
6a2(rgyud): dge ba'am mi dge ba'i las gang 'dod pa bzlas pa tsam gyis byed do//oM ka s+ye span+da swan+da twaM/s+phu n+ya be m+ma ni ma ma tu b+hA span+da ya swa/span+da si ba dza Ni swA hA/oM kA shi swan+da ka she swa nA twaM n+ye shU n+ya pe m+ma ni a sma ka r+N+Na ta thA s+phan+da ya thA s+phan+da si ba dza Na swA hA/mig 'bul bar gyur na byabs 
6a3(rgyud): pas zhi bar byed do//oM dzaM b+hi mo hi hrid ya mA brtA naM hU~M phaT swA hA/sngags kyi rgyal po 'di tho rangs bya rog sgra mi grag pa'i dus su/lan bdun mngon par bsngags pa'i chus gdong bkrus na skye bo thams cad dga' bar 'gyur ro//rgyal la ba mar dang pad+ma dkar po'i skud pa'i 
6a4(rgyud): sdong pos du bslangs la/brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par bsngags pa byas pa des mig la bskus pas bud med thams cad yid 'phrog par byed do//rgyal la kha thur du bltas pa'i me tog gi rtsa ba dang shi ri khaN+Da dag sbyar la/brgya rtsa brgyad mngon bar bsngags pa des thig 
6a5(rgyud): ler byas na rje bo khros pa de rab tu sang bar byed do//ku co dang 'thab pa dang rtsod pa dag las rgyal bar 'gyur ro//cho ga bzhin rtag tu bzlas pas grong khyer bskyod bskyed par byed do//oM staM b+ha ni/staM b+ha ya/dzaM b+ha ni/dzaM b+ha ya/mo ha ni/mo ha ya/rak+Sha ni Ni/rak+Sha ya m+waM/
6a6(rgyud): ba ra di sid+d+ha lo tsa ni swA hA/sangs rgyas spyan gyi snying po 'dis 'jigs pa thams cad sel bar byed do//dpa' bo gcig pu grub pa rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las le'u dang po rdzogs so////rabs dang legs par sangs rgyas pa'i//slob ma yon tan gzhi yin pa//
6a7(rgyud): bla ma la gus mkhas pa la//rgyud kyi rgyal po bshad par bya//oM na mo bi ma la tsan+d+rA ya/de ba su hA ya/sa t+ye na d+h+ri ya ti lo kaHsa t+ye na ba ri gr-i h+ya te sa t+ye na d+harmaHs+thi to lo kaHsa t+ye bra h+ma sa tA na naM/sa t+yaM bud+d+hA sh+tsa d+harma sh+tsa saM g+ha sh+tsa gu Na sA ga raHa ne mas t+yaM bA kye na k+Shi bra mA bi shad rwa 
6b1(rgyud): Ni/tad+yathA/oM sa ra sa ra/si ri si ri/su ru s+ya ru/me ru man+da ra pra ti kA sa A bi sa A bi sha/kai lA sa ku Tu bu twA ya na maHswA hA/ya na ma yA tsi na ti taM kA r+yaM ta ta sarba bru hi ta twA ta/A t+mA nAM bA ba rA naM bA shu b+ham+b+ha ya/di bA shu d+haM/nang par gtsang ba'i sa phyogs su dkyil 'khor byas   
6b2(rgyud): shing bum pa bzhag la/lha'i bu dri ma med pa'i zla ba la mchod pa rgya che ba byas nas me long la mngon par bsngags te/khye'u 'am bu mo legs par khrus byas la gos legs pa'i bkrus pa bskon cing /tsan+dan dkar po'i sde gus yan lag byugs la me tog dri zhim po'i phreng 
6b3(rgyud): ba mgul du btags pa/grong pa'i chos kyis rab tu ma nyams pa la bstan na ji ltar bsams pa'i dgos pa phyin ci ma log par ston par 'gyur ro//dgongs sngas drung du dkyil 'khor nye bar byugs la/bcom ldan 'das dang lha'i bu dri ma med pa'i zla pa la mchod pa 
6b4(rgyud): rgya chen po byas la/brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas nas cang mi smra bar nyal na dge ba dang mi dge ba ji ltar bsams pa bzhin ston par 'gyur ro//rtag tu bzlas na 'jig rten gsum gyi spyod pa ston par 'gyur ro//oM shra pa Na bi shA tsi ni muN+De swAhA/ba ru ra'i shing ngam n+ya gro d+ha'i shing gi rtsa bar  
6b5(rgyud): 'dug la/cang mi smra bar 'bum phrag gcig bzlas na grub par 'gyur te 'jig rten gsum gyi sbyod pa yang ston par 'gyur ro//yang na legs par khrus byas la gos gtsang ma bgos te dben par 'dug la khri bzlas pas grub par 'gyur ro//dgongs nyal kar ru rta la brgya rtsa  
6b6(rgyud): brgyad mngon par bzlas nas gdong dang rkang pa gnyis bkrus na/rmi lam du dge ba dang mi dge brnam par ston par 'gyur ro//o~M na maHsa p+tA nAM/sa mya kaM bud+d+ha/ko TI nAM/tad+ya thA/oM tsa le tsu le/tsun+da swA hA/bI r+ya sa t+ya pA di ni ba ra de ka thA ya swA hA/gong du bshad pa'i tshogs me long 
6b7(rgyud): dmar la gri'am mar me'am chu snod la ston par byed do//lan brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par bsngags pa'i chus gdong bkrus la mi smra bar nyal na rmi lam du dge ba dang me dge ba ston par 'gyur ro//oM mu tsi li swA hA/mo ha ni swA hA/dan ti li swA hA/gsang sngags 'di khri bzlas na 'grub 
7a1(rgyud): @#//par 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la mchod pa rgya chen po byas nas lan brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par bsngags pa'i chus gdong bkrus te/dgos pa yid la byed pa'i bzlas pas mi smra bar nyal na bcom ldan 'das 
7a2(rgyud): rang gi gzugs su mthong bar 'gyur zhing dge ba dang mi dge ba rmi lam du dmigs par 'gyur ro//oM pradz+nyA la hU~M phaT/bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten mgon po'i snying po 'di lan 'bum phrag bcu bzlas na 'grub par 'gyur ro//gong du smos pa'i cho gas dge ba dang mi dge ba rme 
7a3(rgyud): lam du ston par 'gyur ro//oM na maHsa man+ta pra d+hA nAM/a pra ti ga ta shA sa nA nAM/ta d+ya thA/ku mA ra rU pi NaM da r+ShA ya/A t+mA nAM/bi b+hU ri ta med par be b+hU ti li ta sa mud zla ba ya/swa pa nI ni be da ya/ya thA s+thU taM hU~M hU~M phaT phaT swA hA/rje btsun 'jam dpal gzhon nu'i bris sku'am 
7a4(rgyud): lugs ma'i mdun nam sku gdung dang bcas pa'i mchod rten la ci 'byor pa'i mchod pa byas te/brgya phrag bdun bzlas pa byas na rmi lam du bdag nyid dmigs shing gzhan dag gis kyang mthong bar 'gyur ro//oM ka r+ShA bi shA tsi kar+ShA me ka bA ya hU~M phaT/sha za'i shing gi drung gi rtsa 
7a5(rgyud): bar khams gsum rnam par rgyal ba'i ting nge 'dzin la gnas shing /de nyid du mi smra bar 'dug ste/sha dang nya sha dang mi sha dang 'dzam bu ti ka la mngon par bsngags pa'i gtor ma nye bar bstabs la dus gsum du sngags bzlas na/zhag dang po la mtshan ma nye bar ston 
7a6(rgyud): par byed cing phyis 'ong ba 'grub par 'gyur zhing /de la sogs pa srid pa gsum gyi spyod pa rnam par ston par byed do//nA na ga nyi shu rtsa gcig la tshangs pa gsod pa yang 'grub par 'gyur ro//oM tsa tu b+hu dza tsa tu rmu kha Sha Ta yu kha bi kr-i tA na na ka r+Shu pi shA tsi ma A kar+Sha ya hU~M phaT/
7a7(rgyud): dur khrod du gshin rje mthar byed kyi bris sku'i mdun du bzlas pa byas na zhag bdun nas sha za na sgrogs 'grub par 'gyur ro//oM bud+d+ha tsu ru mA tsu ru ke swA hA/sku gdung dang bcas pa'i mchod rten nam rje btsun sangs rgyas kyi spyan sngar bsnyen pa cho ga bri bzlas la/de'i 'og tu ci 'byor pa'i 
7b1(rgyud): mchod pa byas nas nyal ba'i dus su brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas te/bdag gang nas skyes par gyur gang du skye bar 'gyur zhes yid la bsams shing nyal na 'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa'i skye ba dmigs par 'gyur bar the tshom med do//gal te zla ba drug gi nang mo la tu 'chi par 'gyur na de'i tshe rmi 
7b2(rgyud): lam du bdag gam gzhan gyi mgo bo mi mthong bar 'gyur ro//de bzhin du mngon par bsngags pa'i me long bltas pa mkhye'u 'am bu mo gang gi mgo bo mthong bar ma gyur pa de'i zla ba drug gi nang du 'chi bar 'gyur ro//rdo lig bu mig dang shing a gas ti dkar po'i rtsa ba reng bur byas te/
7b3(rgyud): brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par bsngags pas mig la byugs na/gang dag zla ba drug gi nang du 'chibar 'gyur pa'i rkang gnyis dang rkang bzhi de dag thams cad kyi mgo bo med pa mngon sum du mthong bar 'gyur ro/oM swa p+na bi lo ki ni sid d+ha lo tsa ni swa p+naM/me ka thA ya swA hA/sngags 
7b4(rgyud): kyi rgyal po 'di gong nas smos pa'i tshogs las thams cad byed par 'gyur ro//oM a dzi ti de bad ta taM/me de hi da rA pa ya swA hA/chu'i nang du zhugs te bu mo gang gang gi ched du bzlas pa byed pa de de nyid la ster bar byed do//yi ge 'bum bzlas pa byas pa pri yang ku'i me tog 
7b5(rgyud): gam pa la sha'i me tog gi sbyin sreg 'bum gyis dpal chen po grub par byed do//mtshan mo 'o ma can gyis ma shing gis sbyin sreg byas nas/grong gang gi ched du sbyin sreg bzlas pa byed pa de rnyed par 'gyur ro//pad+ma rnams sam bil ba rnams kyi 'bras bu'i sbyin 
7b6(rgyud): sreg 'bum gyis rgyal rigs su skyes pa rgyal srid rnyed par 'gyur zhing gzhan dag gis dpal chen po thob par byed do//ji lta ba bzhin du rnyed pa'i me tog rnams kyi sbyin sreg 'bum phrag bdun gyis 'byor pa mi zad par 'gyur ro//oM dza ye/bi dza ye/a dzi te/a pa rI dza te swA 
7b7(rgyud): hA/sring mo bzhi'i snying po'i sngags kyi rgyal po 'di gong du bshad pa'i cho gas 'bum phrag bzhi bzlas na/gong du bshad pa'i cho gas las thams cad byed par 'gyur ro//oM me g+ho lA ya swA hA/pa la sha'i me tog gam yam shing gis sbyin sreg 'bum phrag gcig byas na gser srang dbul 
8a1(rgyud): @#//rnyed ta ra lu d+ha ra ning bu mo don du gnyer bas kyang gang mngon par 'dod pa'i bu mo rnyed par 'gyur ro//oM ka ma la pA si ni/ka ma li ma hA la k+Sh+mi ra dz+yaM mi de hi pa ra di swA hA/gsang sngags 'di ni dpal chen po'i snying po'o//rtag tu bzlas pa byas na phun sum tshogs 
8a2(rgyud): pa'i mchog ster bar 'gyur ro//ji lta ba bzhin du rnyed pa'i me tog gi sbyin sreg gis dpal chen por 'gyur zhing mngon par 'dod pa'i bumo 'grub par 'gyur ro//ras bal gyi sa bon gyi tshad tsam gu gul gyi ri lu mngar gsum la btags pa'i sbyin sreg 'bum mam pad+ma'i sbyin 
8a3(rgyud): sreg 'bum gyis rgyal srid sgrub par byed do//oM nan tu nar ni ba ra nged ni ba ra nge/gi ni ki Ni khi Ni/ba la khi Ni la khi Ni/shri yaM me dang bau Sha Ta swA hA/dga' byed ma'i sngags kyi rgyal po 'di ni gong du bshad pa'i las thams cad 'grub par 'gyur ro//oM dzam b+he mo ha swA 
8a4(rgyud): hA/rje btsun ma sgrol ma la ka ra bI ra'i me tog gis mngon par mchod de/sngags kyi rgyal po yi ge 'bum bzlas na yid la re ba thams cad rdzogs par byed do//oM ba su d+hA ri Ni swA hA/oM shrI ba su swA hA/oM ba su shrI ye swA hA/oM shr-I ba su mu khI swA hA/oM 
8a5(rgyud): shrI ba su ma ti shrI ye swA hA/bdag nyid dzam+b+ha la'i gzugs su bsgom la/rang gi snying ga'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su ba su d+hA ra gser gyi mdog can phyag gnyis pa rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa/shar phyogs la sogs pa'i phyogs na lha mo bzhis bskor 
8a6(rgyud): pa g.yas kyis mchog sbyin pa/g.yon gyis 'bras kyi snye ma bsnams pa bsgoms la yid la re ba rdzogs par byed do//ba'i lci bas dkyil 'khor gru bzhi khru do pa'i tshad du byas la/dus gsum du me tog dri zhim pos mchod de stong phrag bzhi bzlas pa byas na zla ba drug gis 
8a7(rgyud): yid la re ba thams cad rdzongs par byed do//ji lta ba bzhin du rnyed pa'i me tog gi sbyin sreg 'bum phrag bzhi byas na dpal chen por 'gyur zhing /yi ge 'bum bzlas na dpal chen po 'grub par byed do//gu gul gyi ri lu'i sbyin sreg 'bum byas na/yid la re ba yongs su rdzogs par byed do//oM ga 
8b1(rgyud): su d+hA ri Ni a mu ke ka n+yAM me de hUM/dA pa ya swA hA/sngags 'di chu'i nang du zhugs nas khrus byas te 'bum phrag bzhi nges par bzlas pa byas na/mngon par 'dod pa'i bu mo thob par 'gyur ro//mtshan mo 'o ma can gyi yam shing gis grong gi ming nas smos nas sbyin sreg 'bum byas 
8b2(rgyud): na grong rnyed par 'gyur ro//oM ba su d+hA ri Ni swA hA/oM tsan+d+ra kAn t+yai swA hA/oM dat+tA yai swA hA/oM ba su dat+tA yai swA hA//oM A n+tA yai swA hA/o su b+ha dA yai swA hA/oMgup+tA yai swA hA/o~M dbyi swA hA/o~M swa ra swa t+yai swA hA/gser gyi byang bu'am dngul gyi byang bu'am zangs la'i byang bu'i me tog la 
8b3(rgyud): 'dab ma brgyad pa'i dbus su bsu d+hA ra/sha ra la sogs pa'i 'dab ma la tsan+d+ra kan+tu la sogs pa gnod sbyin mo rnams mngon par bris pa'i byang bu gnyis kha sbyar du byas pa'i nang du mar dang skad cig dkar bcug ste/gong du bshad pa'i tshogs sngar smos pa'i las 
8b4(rgyud): rnams byed par 'gyur ro//dbus su ni oM dzam+b+ha la dza len+d+rA ya swA hA/phyogs su ni oM ma Ni b+ha dA ya swA hA/oM pU r+Shu b+ha dA ya swA hA//oM d+ha na dA ya swA hA/o~M bi sh+la ba NA ya swA hA/mtshams su ni oM ki li mA li swA hA/oM tsi bi kuN+Da li ni swA hA/oM mu khen+d+rA ya swA hA/oM tsa 
8b5(rgyud): ran+d+rA ya swA hA/gnod sbyin gyi rgyal po chen po brgyad kyis yongs su bskor ba'o//bcom ldan 'das ni ba su d+hA ra dang bcas pa gser gyi mdog can/ne'u le dang bI dza sU ra sa bon gyis bkang ba phyag na bsnams pa/gsus pa 'phyang ba/rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan 
8b6(rgyud): pa/gong du bshad pa'i gnod sbyin mo dang /gnod sbyin gyi dbang po dang bcas pa la/dus gsum du mchod pa byas nas/bsgoms shing bzlas pa byas na bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jig rten gsum gyi rgyal srid stsol bar mdzad do//tho rangs chu byor ba brgya rtsa brgyad sbyin pa dang /
8b7(rgyud): bya rog gi skad mi grag par chu'i nang du zhugs te/bzlas pa byas pa de'i gter mi zad cing 'phel bar 'gyur ro//yang na gser la sogs pa'i byang bu la bsu d+hA ra gnod sbyin mo dang bcas pa dang /byang bu gnyis pa la bcom ldan 'das dzam+b+ha la gnod sbyin gyis bskor ba yang dag 
9a1(rgyud): @#//par bris la/kha sbyar du byas te mnyes par byas na dngos grub chen po brgyad kyang ster bar mdzad na/grub pa gzhan lta smos kyang ci dgos/dpa' bo gcig pu grub pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las le'u gnyis pa rdzogs so////oM badzra 
9a2(rgyud): tIk+Sh+Na/duHkha ts+tshe da pra dz+nyA dz+nyA na mUrta ye/dz+nyA na kA ya bA gI shwa ra a ra pa tsa nA ya te na maH/bcom ldan 'das 'jam pa'i rdo rje dpa' bo gcig pu grub ba/ston ka'i zla ba'i 'od ltar dkar ba/phyag g.yon na ut+pal sngon po bsnams pa/g.yas kyis mchog sbyin par bdag 
9a3(rgyud): nyid bsams la/oM A r+ya nud pan na pra kr-i te/pra m+bA swa ra/su bi shud+d+ha/d+ha rma t+tA Na/tris pA tA ta swa ra swa ti bA Ni mu pa la b+ha te/zhag nyi shu rtsa gcig gis dbyangs can gyi ngag thob par 'gyur zhing /zla ba drug gis ngag gi dbang phyug 'grub par byed do//bcom ldan 'das 
9a4(rgyud): ngag gi dbang phyug mngon sum du mthong zhing thos pa 'dzin pa yang thob par 'gyur ro//shu dag zho phyed kyi tshad la lan brgya rtsa brgyad mngon par bsngags pa/tho rangs 'o ma'am mar ram til mar dang lhan cig zla ba gcig 'thungs na glen pa dang dig pa med par byed do//khu byug 
9a5(rgyud): dar ma'i skad dang 'dra bar dbyangs snyan cing tshig gsal bar 'gyur ro//zla ba drug goms par byas na ma thos pa'i bstan bcos mngon du 'gyur zhing /thos pa nam yang brjed par mi 'gyur te thos pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//oM bA kye daM na maH/rje btsun gsung gi dbang phyug 
9a6(rgyud): gi snying po 'di gong ma bzhin ting nge 'dzin la gnas pas goms par byas na gong du bshad pa'i las thams cad byed par 'gyur ro//rgya mtshor 'gro ba'i chu bo la ske ba tsam gyis chu la rgal la bA ti'i me tog gi phun pa 'bum phrag gcig blangs nas sngags brjod cing /
9a7(rgyud): phun pa re re rgyun phyogs su 'bab par 'gyur ba las/phun pa gcig rgyun las bzlog par 'ong ba de so lam reg par mid na thos pa 'dzin pa la sogs stsol bar mdzad do//ji lta ba bzhin rnyed pa me tog gi sbyin sreg 'bum phrag bzhi byam nang gi dbang phyug dang mtshungs par 
9b1(rgyud): 'gyur ro//sman bla ma'i phye ma zho bzhi cam la lan brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas la tho rangs dkyil 'khor byas te/ci 'byor pas bcom ldan 'das 'jam pa'i rdo rje la yang dag par mchod de/mar bam rang skyur dang sbyar nas 'thungs na zla ba dug gis thos pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//tshig 
9b2(rgyud): gi bdag po'i dbyangs snyan par yang skye bar 'gyur ro//sngon du bsnyen pa'i cho gas yi ge 'bum bzlas na sngags kyi rgyal po 'grub par 'gyur ro//phyis zla ba gzas zin pa dang nyi ma gzas zin pa na lcags las byas pa'i ral gri rang gi lag pas blangs te/zla ba mi 
9b3(rgyud): snang ba ji srid grol bar gyur ba de srid bzlas pa byas na ral gri'i rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro/de bzhin du 'khor lo dang /rdo rje dang /rtse gsum pa dang /mda' dang /tho ba dang /zhags pa la sogs pa bsgrub par bya zhing /rig pa la sogs pa dang bcud kyis len la 
9b4(rgyud): sogs pa 'dus byas pa bsgrub par bya'o//yang na mig sman nam thig le 'am rkang pa bsku ba'am dbang du byas pa'i rdzas bsdus te bsgrub par bya'o//'o thug gam na dang zan nam mar dang bcas pa khar dang bcas pas snod bkang ste/lag pas bkab nas bzlas pa 
9b5(rgyud): byas te 'thungs na lo lnga brgyar 'tsho bar 'gyur ro//A sh+la khar bcu gnas bzlas pa byas te ming na sdeb sbyor dang thos pa 'dzin pa dang smra mkhas pa dang yid gzhungs par 'gyur ro//pad+ma'i sbyin sreg 'bum gyis bcom ldan 'das mngon sum du 'gyur ro//bil ba'i sbyin sreg 
9b6(rgyud): 'bum gyis 'jig rten gsum gyi rgyal srid 'grub par byed do//ji lta ba bzhin du rnyed pa'i me tog gi sbyin sreg gis ngag gi dngos grub thob par 'gyur ro//mar la btags pa'i 'bru lnga'i sbyin sreg 'bum phrag bzhi byas pa tsam gyis gnod sbyin mo dang klu'i bu mo la sogs pa dgugs par 
9b7(rgyud): byed do//de bzhin du yam shing lngas kyang bya'o//rtag tu goms par byas na tshe 'di nyid la sangs rgyas nyid kyang bsgrub par byed par 'gyur ro//oM hrIH hU~M/skags kyi rgyal po 'di 'bras bu mnyam por ster bar byed do//oM hrIH ma hA mA yAng ge ma hA swa ra swa t+yai na maH/rje btsun 
10a1(rgyud): @#//'phags ma sgrol ma'i snying po 'dis las thams cad byed par gyur ro//oM pra kr-i ti pa ri shud+d+hA na/sarba d+harmAHpra kr-i t+yA/pa ri shu d+ho ho/sa bA tsA Sh+yan ta ra na ma i ti/zhes bya bas phyi dang nang du rgyu ba dang mi rgyu ba dang bcas pa'i sna tshogs pa dang bdag nyid rang 
10a2(rgyud): bzhin gyis yongs su dag par bsam la/padam dkar po khru bdun pa'i tshad dang de'i steng du zla ba'i dkyil 'khor/de'i steng du phyag gnyis pa padam dang mchog sbyin gyis rnam par mdzes pa/bcom ldan 'das ma thugs rje chen po'i rang bzhin gyi sku can/gcig tu gzhan la phan 'dogs pas 
10a3(rgyud): bzhugs pa/sgrub pa po rnams la mngon par 'dod pa'i dngos grub stsol ba la dgyes pa/ston ka'i zla ba ltar dkar ba tsan+dan dkar po'i lde gus byugs shing me tog gis mdzes par brgyan pa/na bza' dkar po dang mu tig gi phreng bas thugs ka mdzes par 
10a4(rgyud): byas pa/sku rin po che sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa'i sku las yongs su sbos pa'i 'od zer stong gis mtha' yas shing dpag tu med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams snang bar byed pa/lo bcu gnyis kyi na tshod du 'khrungs pa'i sku tsam dag gi dbang phyug ma chen mor bdag nyid rnam par bsam mo//
10a5(rgyud): mdun du shes rab ma dang g.yas su yid gzhungs ma dang /rgyab tu tho ma dang /g.yon du dran pa ma gong du smos pa'i yon tan thams cad kyis mdzes par lha mo rnam par bsam par bya'o//de'i 'og tu lte ba'i phyogs su zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su yi ge oM mdog dkar po 
10a6(rgyud): rnam par bsams la de las byung ba'i ngag gi dbang phyug gi rang bzhin ma lus pa'i phreng ba rgyun mi 'chad pa'i 'byung bar rnam par bsam zhing /gcig pu der phyogs pa'i yid can gyi sngags pas nges par bdag nyid kyi lte ba'i dbus na gnas pa mi sla bas bzlas pa 
10a7(rgyud): byas na/zla ba gcig gis dbyangs can gyi dag rab tu 'thob par 'gyur zhing zla ba gsum gyis tshangs pa gsod pa yang 'grub par 'gyur ro//zla ba drug gis dbyangs can dang ngag mnyam par 'gyur ro//ra yi 'o ma a ru tsha ba gsum//pa tha shu dag se gu sen d+ha ba//lan bdun sngas 
10b1(rgyud): pa'i mar dag ni 'khrungs nad byangs can 'grub par 'gyur//ra yi 'o ma bre bzhi dang //glan rnams srang gcig tsam gyi tshad//mar bre gang dang mkhas pa yis//rim gyis me chung la bskol bya//zla gcig tsam zhig rab sbyar na//bla med tshig ni thob 'gyur zhing //zla drug nye bar longs 
10b2(rgyud): spyod pas//ngag gi dbang phyug mngon sum 'gyur//shu dag shing mngar sen d+ha ba//a ru bca' lga la la phud//ru rta bi pi zi rar bcas//rnam btags ngag ni dag par byed//lha la me tog dri bzangs sogs//nang par yang dag mchod nas ni//lan bdun sngags 
10b3(rgyud): kyis zos nas ni//zla ba drug gis thos 'dzin byed//zla ba gsum gyis tshig bdag 'gyur//zla ba gcig gis blo ldan zhing //bu byug gzhon nu'i skad bzhin du//dbyangs snyan pa ni skye bar 'gyur//gangs can skyes pa'i ma ru 'am//gangs can skyes pa'i

15a6(rgyud): ba yi//shin tu dangs par dkar nag phyed pa'i spyan//smin tshugs mdzes pas zla ba tshes ltar 'khyug/mig 'dzums zur gyis lta bas byams pa'i rtags//sil snyan rnam dag mtshan rab 'bru chags pa//rin chen ma lus rnam pa du mas spras//rgyal bas zhing mang rnam 
15a7(rgyud): pa sna tshogs su//rnam thar dpag yas chos sgo grangs med gnas//shangs kyi dbyibs legs skye bo yongs las 'phags//mtho la yid tsam zur la legs pa'i mtshan//drang la chu phran 'dra bar mdzes pa'i tshul//bzang legs bskal pa brgyar yang bltar mi ngoms//
15b1(rgyud): pad+ma ye brgan tshon ltar dmar ba'i spos//yid gnubs sel ba ma byil ba bzang 'begs sku//mdog sdug chu skyes snang ba mtsho lo bzhin//shin tu dang ba'i yid kyis 'dzum pa mdzad//zhal ras zla 'dra gser gyi mdog gis rgyas//'od kyi dkyil 'khor gser phreng 
15b2(rgyud): du mas spras//gsal ba'i 'od phung nyi ma brgya pas lhag/'od zer sna tshogs sprin phung bzhin du 'phro//chos dbyings 'jam pa'i dbyangs kyis sgra sgrogs pa//ka la bing ka lta bur yid 'phrog mdzad//sang sang dbyangs rab gsung dbyangs rab bsgrags pa//
15b3(rgyud): seng ge ring rog ser gyi dri ma ltar 'khrol//skyes mchog mdzad pa sgam par mdzad pa'i sku//cha lugs bzhin 'chang gzi brjid 'phags pa'i sku//gzhon nu'i tshul chags shin tu gzhon par gda'//gdul bya'i skye bo gzhon nu'i gzugs kyis gdul//dbus 
15b4(rgyud): rgyan rin chen rnam pa sna tshogs dang //mu tig do shal gser gyi rgyan du bkod//pad+ma lha yi na bza' sil ma bsnams//g.yas na mtshon mchog bsnams pa thabs kyis 'dul//dge med rno ba shes rab nyid du nges//chos dbyings tshul nyid yang dag brjod pa dang //
15b5(rgyud): mi shigs rdo rje gtan nas 'bigs pa'i tshul//g.yon pa shes rab pha rol phyin pa dang //blang dor gnyis med bsam gyis mi khyab dbyings//zab mo'i chos nyid thugs su chud pa'i don//bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jam dpal rnon po la bstod pa rdzogs so////
15b6(rgyud): 'jam dpal ngag gi dbang phyug la bu mo brgyad kyis bstod pa//gzhon nu'i sku lus 'chang ba po//ye shes sgron mes rab brgyan zhing //'jig rten gti mug mun sel ba//'jam pa'i dbyangs la phyag 'tshal lo//nyid kyi mdangs bzang tshogs kyis ni//
15b7(rgyud): zla ba rgyas pa zil gnon zhing //ma rig rnam par bsal gyur pa//'jam pa'i dpal la phyag 'tshal lo//ma bgyis zur phud gsal ba la//nor bu 'bar ba'i sku mnga' zhing //nyid ni kun du dga' ba'i sku//'jam dpal khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//bdud 
16a1(rgyud): @#//rtsi 'dra zhing 'jam la phab//yid 'ong gsung gi sgra sgrogs pa//dam pa chos kyi rin chen mdzod//'jam dpal khyod la phyag 'tshal bstod//gang gA'i chu thigs ltar grangs med//'jig rten khams kun ma lus par//nyon mongs dri ma rab 
16a2(rgyud): spangs pa'i//'jam dpal khyod la phyag 'tshal bstod//mche ba gtsigs shing bsgyings pa yi//'jigs pa'i sku yi tshul gyis ni//gshin rje'i dregs pa 'joms mdzad pa//'jam dpal khyod la byag 'tshal bstod//rang gi spyan dang mdangs 'dra ba'i//ut+pal sngon 
16a3(rgyud): pos rab mdzes pa//lhung bzed phyag na bsnams pa yi//'jam dpal khyod la baphyag 'tshal lo//phyogs bcur bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas la//rgya cher mchod dang bstod pa yis//grol ba'i lam ni ston pa po//'jam dpal khyod la phyogs 'tshal lo//bu mo brgyad kyis bstod 
16a4(rgyud): pa rdzogs so//@#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya pra dz+nyA bA ra mi tA nA mA Sh+Ta sha ta kaM/bod skad du/'phags pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad pa zhes bya ba/bcom ldan 'das ma shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa la phyag 'tshal lo//'das dang ma byon da ltar gyi//
16a5(rgyud): rgyal ba gang lags de kun gyi//yum ni dge ma khyod lags te//chod ni lha mo rgyal ba'i sras//rang bzhin med pa'i rang bzhin can//sangs rgyas yum gyur de yi mtshan//dge ma bdag gis brjod par bgyi//'byor bar 'dod pas mnyan par gyis//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa

16a7(rgyud): dang /stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa nyid dang /smon pa med pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa nyid dag ngo bo nyid dang /dngos po medapa'i ngo bo nyid dang /chos nyid dang /chos kyi dbyings dang /chos gnas pa nyid dang /chos mi 'gyur ba nyid dang /chos skyon med pa nyid dang /chos kyi mtshan nyid 
16b1(rgyud): dang /chos bdag med pa nyid dang /ngo bo nyid med pa nyid dang /sems can med pa nyid dang /srog med pa nyid dang /gsog med pa nyid dang /skyes bu med pa nyid dang /gang zag med pa nyid dang /brjod par bya ba dang /brjod par bya bam yin pa dang /sems dang yang dang rnam par shes pa dang bral ba dang /
16b2(rgyud): mnyam pa med pa dang /mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa dang /bdag gi ba med pa dang /rgyags pa med pa dang /spros pa med pa dang /spros ba dang bral ba dang /spros pa thams cad dang bral ba dang /spros ba thams cad las shin tu 'das pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yum dang /byang chub sems 
16b3(rgyud): dpa' thams cad skyed ba dang /nyan thos thams cad 'dzin pa dang /rang sangs rgyas thams cad skrun pa dang /'jig rten thams cad 'dzin pa dang bsod nams kyi tshogs zad mi shes pa bsags pa dang /ye shes 'bab pa dang /rdzu 'phrul mdzad pa dang /lha'i mig rnam par sbyong bar mdzad 
16b4(rgyud): pa dang /lha'i rna ba rab tu dang bar mdzad pa dang /pha rol gyi sems shes par mdzad pa dang /sngon gyi gnas rjes su dran par mdzad pa dang /'chi 'pho ba dang /skye ba shes par mdzad pa dang /nyon mongs pa thams cad zad par mdzad pa dang /'phags pa dang /gtsang ba dang /bsangs ba dang /
16b5(rgyud): dran pa nye bar gzhag pa la gnas pa dang /yang dag par spong ba'i shugs dang ldan pa dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi'i zhabs dang ldan pa dang /dbang po sbyong bar mdzad pa dang /stobs mdzad pa dang /kha na ma tho ba med pa dang /byang chub kyi yan lag bdun gyi rin po ches mdzes par 
16b6(rgyud): byas pa dang /nor bdun sbyin par mdzad pa dang /'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa ston pa dang /mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu ston par mdzad pa dang /dbang bcu sgrub pa dang /sa bcu la gnas par mdzad pa dang /stob sa bcu yongs su rdzogs par 
16b7(rgyud): mdzad pa dang /zad par gyi skye mched bcus mdzes par byas pa dang /shes pa bcu sgrub pa dang /bag la nyal ba chu'i dgra 'jig par mdzad pa dang /bsam gtan rnams sgrub par mdzad pa dang /gzugs med pa'i khams las yang dag par 'da' bar mdzad pa dang /yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs 
17a1(rgyud): @#//rgyas thams cad kyis bstod pa dang /shes pa thams cad mdzad pa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad rdzogs par mdzad pa dang /nang stong pa nyid dang /phyi stong pa nyid dang /phyi nang stong pa nyid dang /stong pa nyid stong pa nyid dang /chen po stong pa nyid dang /don dam pa stong pa nyid dang /'dus byas stong pa nyid dang /'du ma byas stong pa 
17a2(rgyud): nyid dang /mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid dang /ngor ba med pa stong pa nyid dang rang bzhin stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad stong pa nyid dang /rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid dang /mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa stong pa nyid dang ri bo nyid stong pa nyid dang /
17a3(rgyud): ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid dang ma byung ba dang /ma skyes pa dang /ma 'gags pa dang /chad pa med pa dang /rtag pa med pa dang /don gcig pa ma yin pa dang /don tha dad pa ma yin pa dang /'ong ba med pa dang /'gro ba med pa dang /rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba rnam par bsgom pa dang /rab tu brtag par bya ba ma yin pa dang /rnam

17a5(rgyud): pa dang /gnyen po med pa dang /ma gos pa dang /nam mkha'i lam dang 'dra ba dang /yod pa dang med par brjod pa ma yin pa dang /rmi lam gyi ngo bo nyid dang /sgyu ma lta bu'i chos nyid dang /mgal me bskor ba dang 'dra bar chos thams cad ro gcig pa nyid do//su zhig shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i mtshan brgya rtsa brgyad pa 'di 
17a6(rgyud): 'don pa de don song thams cad las rab tu thar bar 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas thams cad kyis kyang dgongs par 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis kyang rtag par rgyun du srung ba dang /skyob pa dang /srid pa byed par 'gyur ro//de'i gsang sngags kyi tshig 'di dag kyang rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par dran par 
17a7(rgyud): bya'o//bcom ldan 'das ma 'gags ma shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi nam/mos pa la byams ma/yon tan dpag tu med pa dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes kyis rnam par mkhyen pa nyid/sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' thamasacad kyi phyag bzhes pa la phyag 'tshal lo//'di lta 
17b1(rgyud): ste/oM pradz+nyo pradz+nyo ma hA be Sh+Te/pradz+nyA+e a brdA se/pradz+nyA mA lo ka ka re/adz+nyA na bi d+ya ma ne/si di d+hi/su si da d+hi/sid d+h+ya ma ne/b+ha ga ba ti sa rbAM ga bu na dA ri/b+ha ga ba ti/brtas li/pra sA ri ta da ste/sa mA sh+tsAs ka ri/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/ka ma pa ka ma pa/tsa la tsa la/rA ba rA ba/gardzA ga rdzA/A ga ts+tsha/A gats+tsha/b+ha ga ba ti mA bi la m+ba swA hA/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dzin

17b6(rgyud): gnyis ma yin par yang ma yin/mtshan mar ma yin/mtshan ma ma yin par yang ma yin /blang bar yang ma yin/dor bar yang ma yin/bsnan par ma yin/bsnan pa med par yang ma yin/kun nas nyon mongs par ma yin/kun nas nyon mod sa ba ma yin par yang ma yin/rnam par byang bar ma yin/rnam par 
17b7(rgyud): byang ba ma yin par yang me yin/spang bar ma yin/spang ba ma yin par yang ma yi na/gnas su ma yin/gnas su ma yin par yang ma yin/sbyor bar ma yin/sbyor ba ma yin par yang ma ma yin/'brel bar ma yin par yang ma yin/rkyen du ma yin/rkyen ma yin par yang ma yin/chos su ma yin/chos ma 
18a1(rgyud): @#//yin par yang ma yin/de bzhin nyid ma yin/de bzhin nyid mayin par yang ma yin/yang dag pa'i mthar ma yin/yang dag pa'i mkhar ma yin par yang ma yin no//kau shi ka de bzhin du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i don ni 'di lta ste/chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa nyid mnyam mo//
18a2(rgyud): chos thams cad zad pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol? tu phyin pa za ba po//chos thams cad dben pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha ral tu phyin pa dben no//chos thams cad g.yo ba med pa'i phyir shes rab kyi phar rol tu phyin pa g.yo ba med do//chos thams cad rlom sems med pa'i phyir shes rab kyis pha rol tu phyin pa rlom sems med 
18a3(rgyud): do//chos thams cad 'jigs pa med pa'i phyir shes rab kyi'i pha rol tu phyin pa 'jigs pa med do//chos thams can bag tsha ba med pa'i phyir shes rab? kyi pha rol tu phyin pa bag tsha ba med do//chos thams chad ro gcig pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pharol tu phyin pa ro gcig go/chos thams cad skye ba med pa'i phyir shes rab?? kyi pha rol tu phyin pa skye ba 
18a4(rgyud): med do//chos thams cad/bag tsha ba med do//chos? thams cad nam mkha' lta bu'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa nam mkha' lta bu'o//gzugs mu med pa'i phyir shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa mu med do//de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par 
18a5(rgyud): shes pa mu med pa'i phyir shes rabakyi pha rol tu phyin pa mu med/do//sa'i khams mu med pa'i phyir shes ra ba kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mu myed do//de bzhin du chu'i khams dang /me'i khams dang /rlung gi khams dang /nam mkha'i khams dang /rnam par shes pa'i khams mu med pa'i phyir/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na ba mu med do//ri rab 
18a6(rgyud): mu med pa'i phyir shes rabagyi pha rol tu phyin pa mu mdo'o//rgya mtsho mu med pa'i phyir shes rarab kyi pha rol tu phyin ba mu med do//rdo rje mnyam pa'i phyir shes rab kyi pha rol? tu phyin pa mnyam mo//chos thams cad dbyer med pa'i phyir shes rab? gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dbyer med do//chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid dmigs su med pa'i phyir

20a1(rgyud): @#//gsum bskor ba byas te phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//gzhan yang 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong snyed kyang de na 'dus par gyur te/thams cad kyang klu'i rgyal po chen po ltar gzir rjid kyi byin gyi mthu che ba/shin tu

73b7(rgyud): gyur na bshad par bya////sdug bsngal rgya mtsho las ni sems can dgrol///pha rol phyin pa drug ni rdzogs par bya//byang chub bla med phyi nas thob par shog//dgra med zhing ni bdag gis thob par shog/rnga rab byin pa'i rnam smin 'bras bu dang //
74a1(rgyud): @#//rgyal ba thams cad bstod pa'i rgyu yi phyir//shAkya thub pa'i dbang po mngon mthong shog/bdag gis lung bstan pa yang der thob shog/gang 'di bdag gi bu pho gnyis po dag/gser gyi dpung pa'i dbang dang gser gyi 'od//khye'u

120a4(rgyud): rnams kyis ni srung bar byed//mi yi 'jig rten skyes gyur kyang //gtso mchog gyur phyir lha zhes bya//lha rnams kyis ni bsrungs pas na//lha yi bu zhes brjod pa yin//sum cu rtsa gsum bdag po yis//stobs bgos mi rgyal stongs par byed//lha ni mang 
120a5(rgyud): por thams cad kyis//rang dbang stobs ni rab bstangs nas//chos min kun ni rab bsal te//sdig pa'i las ni skyed mi byed//sems can dge ba sgrub bskul nas//mtho ris gnas su skye bar byed//mi dang lha min 'khor dag dang //dri za dang ni srin 
120a6(rgyud): po dang //gdod pa la sogs thams cad kyis//stobs kyi byed ni stong par byed//da bsams stobs byed bstangs nas ni//sdig spong dge ba sgrub la 'dzud//lha rnams lhan cig rab bsrungs nas//dge sdig rnam smin ston par byed//dge sdig las 
120a7(rgyud): ni byas gyur na//da ltar tshe 'di nyid la ni//lha rnams lhan cig rang bsrungs nas//dge sding rnam smin ston par byed//yul mis sdig pa'i las byas la//rgyal spangs nan tur mi byed na//'di ni yang dag rigs pa min//chos bzhin chad pas gcad par 
120b1(rgyud): @#//gal te nyes mthong mi 'gog na//chos min rab tu 'phel 'gyur bas//rgyal po de yi yul khams su//g.yo sgyu mang du 'phel bar 'gyur//rgyal pos nyes byed mthong ba la//gal te zlog par mi byed na//sum cu rtsa gsum lha 'khor rnams//
120b2(rgyud): thams cad khong khro'i sems skye 'gyur//rgyu des rgyal khrims nyams 'gyur zhing //g.yo sgyu 'jig rten 'phel 'gyur bas//pha rol dgra 'khor dag gis ni//yul de bcom nas 'jig par byed//gnas pa'i khyim dang yo byad dang //nor bsags 
120b3(rgyud): thams cad stor bar 'gyur//g.yo sgyu sna tshogs skyes pas na//phan tshun gcig la gcig kyang 'phrog/yang dag chos kyis rgyal thob kyang //chos bzhin du ni mi spyod pas//yul mi thams cad 'khor 'gyur te//glang pos pad+ma'i rjid brdzis 'dra//
120b4(rgyud): rlung drag ldang la dus kyang med//mi 'phrod char ni dus min 'bab//rgyu skar lhas ngan mang po ston//nyi zla gcan zin 'od kyang med//rgyal pos yang dag chos spangs pas//'phru rnams me tog 'bas bu dang //lo tog thams cad mi smin cing //yul na 
120b5(rgyud): mu ge'i byung bar 'gyur//rgyal pos yang dag chosaspangs nas//sdig chos mi ni ston byed na//gzhal med khang gnas lha rnams kyis//mthong nas mya ngan skye bar 'gyur//lha rgyal 'khor bcas de dag gis//'di skad du ni smra bar 'gyur//rgyal 
120b6(rgyud): 'dis chos min rab spyad nas//sdig pa'i grogs po bsten pas na//rgyal srid yun ring brtan mi 'gyur//lha rnams thams cad rab mi dga'//de dag khros par gyur pa'i phyir//yul de 'jig par 'gyur zhes zer//chos min mi ni rab brten pas//
120b7(rgyud): yul khams 'phel bar 'gyur bas na//'thab rtsod g.yo sgyu mang por 'gyur//rims nad sdug bsngal mang po skye//lha yi bdag po srung mi byed//lha gzhan thams cad spong bar byed//de yi yul ni rang zhig nas//rgyal po sdug bsngal myong bar 
121a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur//pham chu dam bu rnams dang //phu nu dang ni sring mo rnams//cig car sdug dang bral 'gyur zhing //tha mar bdag kyang 'chi bar 'gyur//ltas ngan skar mda' dag kyang ltung //nyi ma gnyis ni cig car 'char//pha rol dgra 
121a2(rgyud): ni nyer 'ongs pas//yul mi 'chi bar 'khrug phrad 'gyur//yul bkur blon po chen po rnams//nyes med glo bur 'chi bar 'gyur//glang po rta sogs gces pa rnams//thams cad 'thor zhing stongs par 'gyur//yul yul kun du dgra yod 
121a3(rgyud): pas//mi ni phal cher dus min 'chi//'dre srin mang po yul zhugs pas//rims nad kun du 'phel bar 'gyur//yul mi blon po chen po dang //nye ba na mang po thams cad kyang //sems ni g.yo sgyu bcas pas na//thams cad chos min spyod par 'gyur//
121a4(rgyud): chos min spyod pa mthong gyur na//snying du sdug cing bkur bar byed//dge chos spyod pa'i mi dag la//sdug bsngal nyon mongs chad pas gcod//ma rungs byams shing bkur ba dang //dge ba chad pas bcad pa'i phyir//gza' skar rlung dang char pa 
121a5(rgyud): rnams//thams cad du kha su rgyu mi 'gyur//nyes dmigs rnam gsum skye bar 'gyur//yang dag chos ni nub 'gyur zhing //sems can kha dog 'di mi ldan//sa yi bcud ni thur du 'bab//sdig pa kun dge la brnyas pas na//gzhan yang nyes dmigs 
121a6(rgyud): rnam gsum 'byung //dus min sad dang ser ba 'bab//mu ge'i sdug bsngal kun du khyab//lo tog 'bras bu mang po rnams//thams cad re zhim nyams par 'gyur//de dag gi ni yul khams na//sems can phal cher na bar 'gyur//de yi yul na ljon shing 
121a7(rgyud): rnams//sngon chad 'bras bu zhim chags pa//de yi dbang gis nyams 'gyur bas//kha rtsub ro mchog med par 'gyur//sngon gyi kun dga' nags tshal mchog/snying du sdug cing rtse ba'i gnas//thams cad skams shing rnyings 'gyur bas//mthong 
121b1(rgyud): ba kun gyis mya ngan skye//sA yu na sa dang lo tog rnams//ro zhim khad kyis med gyur pas//bza' tshe sems kyang mi dga' na//'byung chen 'phel byed ga la nus//sems can kha dog 'od nyams shing //mthu stobs zad cing nyams par 'gyur//kha zas mang 
121b2(rgyud): po zos kyang ni//'grangs pa nyid du yong mi 'gyur//yul khams de na yod pa yi//sems can rnam pa ji snyed pa//stobs chung dpa' bshor med pas na//bya ba dag ni byed mi nus//yul mi phal cher nad thebs pas//sdug bsngal sna tshogs lus 
121b3(rgyud): gzir 'gyur//'dre srin kun du khyab pas na//thams cad kun du srin por skye//rgyal pos chos min rab spyad nas//mi dge'i grogs po bsten pas na//'jig rten rnam pa gsum po kun//des na gnod pa sna tshogs myong //de 'dra'i nyes dmigs 
121b4(rgyud): mtha' med pa//yul nas 'byung bar 'gyur ba yang //sdig can mi ni mthong 'gyur yang //yal por ma bskrad pa las gyur//lha rnams kun gyis byin brlabs nas//yul gyi rkyal po byas gyur kyang //yang dag dam pa'i chos dag gis//yul 
121b5(rgyud): khams yongs su srung mi byed//gang zhig dge spyod sgrub byed na//rtag tu mtho ris gnas su skye//gal te sdig pa'i las byas pa//tshe 'phos nges par ngan 'gror ltung //gal te rgyal pos yul mi rnams//thal rangs nyes pa byed 'jug na//
121b6(rgyud): sum cu rtsa gsum lha tshogs rnams//thams cad rab gdungs sems skye 'gyur//lha rnams dang ni pha ma yi//bstan dag bzhin du mi byed pa//'di ni chos min mi yin pas//rgyal po sri zhu'i bu ma yin//gal te rang gi yul khams su//chos min 
121b7(rgyud): spyod pa mthong ba'i tshe//chos bzhin chad pas gcod byed cig/yal ngor sems ni mi bskyed na//de phyir lha tshogs mang po kun//rgyal po de la skyob par byed//byang chub spyod pa bsgrub pa'i phyir//sdig pa'i chos rnams zhi byed na//rgyal po tshe 'di nyid la ni//
122a1(rgyud): @#//mthong ba'i rnam smin nges par myong //dge dang mi dge'i las dag la//spyod spong sems can bskul nas ni//dge sdig rnam smin bstan pas na//de phyir mi rgyal thob nas kyang //lha rnams lhan cig byin brlabs nas//thams cad mi 
122a2(rgyud): rang 'gyur ba yin//bdag dang gzhan la phan gdags phyir//yang dag chos kyis yul bskyang bya//g.yo sgyu byed pa mthong gyur na//chos bzhin chad pas gcad par bya//gal te rgyal thabs stor ba dang //srog 'bab rkyen zhig byung na yang //nams 
122a3(rgyud): kyang sdig chos mi spyod cing //sdig pa mthong na spang bar bya//dgos pa'i nang na mchog lci bas//rgyal srid ster las lhag pa med//'di kun g.yo sgyu mi las gyur//des na chad pas gcad par bya//g.yo sgyu byed pa yod gyur na//rgyal srid dag kyang 
122a4(rgyud): stor 'gyur zhing //rgyal srid dag kyang nyams 'gyur bas//glang chen me tog tshal zhugs 'dra//de yis mi rgyal byas kyang ni//chos kyis yul ni ma bskyangs pas//lha dbang thams cad mi dga' zhing //lha ma yin yang de bzhin no//de lta bas na chos bzhin 
122a5(rgyud): du//sdig pa byed pa chad pas gcad//dge bas sems can gdul bya zhing //chos min rjes su 'brang mi bya//lus dang srog ni spang sla yi//mi dge grogs rjes 'brang mi bya//nye ba dang ni nyo min pa//thams cad mnyam pa nyid du blta//yang dag chos 
122a6(rgyud): kyis rgyal byed na//yul mi mi drang med pa'i phyir//chos rgyal grags dang ldan 'gyur bas//khams gsum kun du grags par 'gyur//sum cu rtsa gsum lha tshogs rnams//dga' nas 'di skad smra bar 'gyur//'dzam gling chos rgyal gang yin pa//
122a7(rgyud): de ni bdag gi bu yin te//dge bas sems can 'dul byed cing //yang dag chos kyis yul bskyangs nas//yang dag chos spyod bskul bas na//bdag cag gnas su skye 'gyur zer//lha dang mi yi bu rnams dang //lha ma yin gyi 'khor rnams kyang //
122b1(rgyud): rgyal pos yang dag chos 'dul rgyus//rtag tu sems ni dga' bar 'gyur//lha tshogs nyams pa dga' nas//mi rgyal de la srung bar byed//gza' skar rang gi lam rgyu nyid//nyi zla tshad las 'da' mi byed//rlung ni rtag par dus bzhin ldang //bdud 
122b2(rgyud): rtsi'i char ni dus bzhin 'bab//lo tog thams cad smin lags pas//mi rnams bu gi legs par 'gyur//lha tshogs mar ba thams cad bum//rang gi pho brang gang 'gyur bas//de phyir lo rje khyod kyis ni//lus spangs lang dag chos spel byos//rin chen chos 
122b3(rgyud): la bkur byed na//des na mang po bde 'gyur bas//rtag par yang dag chos brtson nas//yon tan bdag nyid brgyan par bya//'khor rnams rtag par rab dga' zhing //sdig pa rnams ni spong bar byed//chos kyis sems can 'dul byed pas//rgyun du bde ba 
122b4(rgyud): 'thob par 'gyur//mi rnams de dag thams cad kun//dge ba bcu ni spyod byed na//yul khams rtag par lo legs shing //yul ni zhib nyid du 'gyur//rgyal po chos kyis mi 'dul zhing //dge ba sa sdig spyod 'dul byed na//snyan pa'i grags 
122b5(rgyud): pa 'thob 'gyur zhing //sems can rnams ni bde bar byed//de nas de'i chos chen po la gnas pa'i mi'i rgyal po thams cad dang 'khor mang po rnams bcom ldan 'das kyis sngon gyi mi'i rgyal pos yul skyong ba'i chos kyi don bkas bshad pa thob 
122b6(rgyud): pas thams cad ngo mtshar rmad du gyur pa dang /rab tu dga' ba thob par gyur pas shin tu dang ba'i mngon par yid ches pa dag gis bka' yang dag par blangs te spyod par gyur to//gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po las rgyal po'i 
122b7(rgyud): chos lugs yang dag pa'i bstan bcos bstan pa'i le'u ste nyi shu pa'o////gser 'od dam pa mchog du rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde rgyal po/bam po dgu pa/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 'khor mang po rnams la rgyal po'i chos lugs yang dag pa'i 
123a1(rgyud): @#//bcos bshad nas yang 'khor mang po rnams la bka' stsal pa//byed la sogs pa legs par nyon cig dang /ngas der khyod la sngon chos yang dag par spyod pa'i rgyu dang rkyen bshad par bya'o//de nas de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de bka' 
123a2(rgyud): stsal pa/ngas sngon 'khor los sgyur rgyal byas pa na//sa chen 'di dang rgya mtshor bcas pa btang //gling bzhi rin chen gyis ni rab bkar ba//thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la mchod//ngas sngon bskal pa dag ni tshad med snying //rnam dag yang dag chos 
123a3(rgyud): sku gnyer ba'i phyir//phangs pa'i nor dang rang gi lus grog kyang //ser sna med pa'i sems kyis yongs su btang //gzhan yang 'das pa'i bskal pa bsam dka' na//yang dag mkhyen pa rin chen gtsug phud byung //de bzhin gshegs de mya ngan 'das pa'i 'og/
123a4(rgyud): rgyal po legs skyes zhes bya 'jig rten byung //'khor los sgyur rgyal byas nas gling bzhi btul//rgya mtsho'i mthar thug tshun chad skyabs su dong //grong khyer ming ni gya nom sgra zhes bya//de tshe 'khor los sgyur rgyal de na gnas//rmi lam sangs rgyas bsod 
123a5(rgyud): nams ye shes thos//chos smra rin chen brtsegs pa zhes bya ba//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor lhar mdzes stan 'dug nas//gser 'od dam pa'i mdo sde 'chad pa'ang mthong //de tshe rgyal de rmi lam sad pa dang //rab tu dga' ba skyes nas lus kun khyab//nam langs 
123a6(rgyud): rgyal po'i pho brang phyir byung nas//dge slong dge 'dun rnams kyi thad du song //'phags pa'i tshogs la gus pas rab mchod nas//tshogs chen mang po de la 'di skad dris//chos sgra rin chen brtsegs pa zhes bgyi ba//yon tan phun tshogs sems can 'dul 
123a7(rgyud): mchis sam//de tshe chos smra chen po rin chen brtsegs//gnas khang gcig pur yang dag gnas nas ni//dran pa nyer gzhag mdo sde 'di bton pas//drang 'dug mi g.yo lus dang sems kyang bde//de tshe dge slong zhig gis rgyas khrid nas//rin chen 
123b1(rgyud): brtsegs bde yi gnas su phyin//gnas khang nang du lus ni drang ba drang ste//'od dang mtshan mchog lus kun khyab par mthong //rgyal po de la 'di ni rin chen brtsegs//mdo sde'i rgyal po mchog gyur 'di lta ste//gser 'od dam pa shin tu gya nom mchog/sangs 
123b2(rgyud): rgyas spyod yul zab mo 'dzin ces smras//rgyal pos rin chen brtsegs la phyag byas nas//gus pas thal mo sbyar nas 'di skad smras//zla ba nya ltar zhal mdog mdzes gyur pa//gser 'od dam pa'i chos 'di bshad du gsol//rin chen brtsegs kyis rgyal po'i 
123b3(rgyud): tshig mnyan nas//gser 'od dam pa 'di ni bshad gnang bas//stong gsum 'jig rten khams 'di kun khyab pa'i//lha mi thams cad rab tu dga' bar 'gyur//rgyal pos sa phyogs yangs shing gtsang ma'i gnas//rin chen gya nom mchog gis rab brgyan te//
123b4(rgyud): spos chu dam pa dag gis chag chag btab//me tog sna tshogs dag ni gcal du bkram//gnas mchog der ni stan mtho rab bting ste//ba dan bla re bres nas mdzes par byas//bye ma byug spos rnam pa sna tshogs kyis//bdug pa dag ni kun du khyab par byas//
123b5(rgyud): lha klu lha ma yin dang mi'am ci//lto 'phye chen po dang ni gnod sbyin dang //lha rnams man dA ra ba'i char phab nas//thams cad nyer 'ongs snyan mchog de la mchod//gzhan yang lha ni 'bum phrag brgya stong rnams//dam chos nyan mos thams cad der ni 'dus//
123b6(rgyud): chos smra dang po stan las langs pa'i tshe//thams cad lha yi me tog dag gis mchod//de tshe chos sgra chen po rin chen brtsegs//shin tu lus bkrus gos gtsang rab bgos te//tshogs chen chos stan de drung song nas ni//thal sbyar rtse gcig sems kyis 
123b7(rgyud): phul thos byas//lha dbang lha tshogs lha yi bu mo rnams//thams cad me tog man dA ra yang gtor//lha yi rol mo brgya stong bsam mi khyab//mkha' la gnas nas sgra dbyangs snyan par phyung //de tshe chos smra chen po rin chen brtsegs//stan la 'dug ste skyil mo krung bcas nas//
124a1(rgyud): @#//phyogs bcu'i zhing na bzhugs pa'i byams chen gtso//bye ba brgya stong de dag yid la bsams//sdug bsngal sems can thams cad ji snyed pa//thams cad byams sems mnyam pa nyer gzhag nas//gsol 'debs bdag po rgyal po legs pa'i 
124a2(rgyud): phyir//gser 'od dam pa'i mdo sde rab tu bshad//rgyal pos de 'dra'i chos ni thos gyur nas//thal sbyar rtse gcig rjes su yi rang smras//ya mtshon chos thos mchi ma blags te dus//lus dang sems ni dga' chen kun khyab gyur//de tshe yul bdag rgyal po 
124a3(rgyud): legs skyes kyis//mdo sde 'di ni mchod par 'dod pa'i phyir//lag na yid bzhin nor bu thogs nas ni//sems can kun phyir smon lam 'di skad btab//'dzam gling 'di ni kun du khyab par yang //rin chen rgyan phreng char ni rab phab nas//nor dang yo byad rgud phongs 
124a4(rgyud): ji snyed pa//thams cad yid bzhin bde ba myong bar shog/de nas rin chen sna bdun char phab nas//gling bzhi kun du chog cing tshim byas pas//lus kyi rgyan phreng gos dang zas skom dang //ci dang ci 'dod phongs pa med par gyur//de tshe yul bdag rgyul po legs skyes kyis//
124a5(rgyud): gling bzhi rin chen char bab mthong ba dang //thams cad sangs rgyas rin chen gtsug phud kyi//bstan la zhugs pa'i dge slong dge 'dun mchod//'das pa'i rgyal po legs skyes gang yin pa//shAkya thub pa nga yin shes par byos//sngon yang sa chen mang po rab btang 
124a6(rgyud): ste//rin chen dag gis gling bzhi yongs su bkang //sngon tshe chos mkhan chen po rin chen brtsegs//legs skyes de la gya nom chos bshad de//mdo sde'i rgyal po de dag bshad pa'i phyir//shar phyogs sangs rgyas mi 'khrugs grub pa yin//ngas ni mdo 
124a7(rgyud): sde'i rgyal po 'di mnyan nas//thal sbyar rtse gcig yi rang brjod pa dang //rin chen sna bdun btang ba'i bsod nams kyis//rdo rje sku mchog 'di ni brnyes pa yin//gser 'od bsod nams brgya yi mtshan brgyan pas//mthong ba thams cad kun ni rab dga' zhing //
124b1(rgyud): sems can thams cad kun gyi snying du sdug/lha tshogs mang po bye ba kun de bzhin//'das tshe bskal pa bye ba dgu bcu dgu//'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po dag kyang byas//yul khams chung ngur mi yi rgyal po byas//gzhan yang bskal pa brgya stong tshad med snying //
124b2(rgyud): brgya byin lha yi dbang po dag kyang byas//tshangs pa'i rgyal po chen po dag byas te//phyogs bcu'i byams chen gtso la mchod pa yi//de yi grangs tshod shin tu mthar thug dka'//ngas sngon mdo thos rjes su yi rang dge/bsod nams phung po ci yod tshad shes dka'//bsod 
124b3(rgyud): nams de dbang byang chub mngon rtogs nas//chos sku yang dag ye shes dam pa brnyes//de nas 'khor mang po rnams de skad ces bka' stsal pa thos pas ngo mtshar rmad du gyur nas thams cad kyis mdo sde gser 'od dam pa spel zhing rgyun mi 'chad par spyad par mos 
124b4(rgyud): par gyur to//gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po las rgyal po legs skyes kyi le'u ste nyi shu gcig pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dpal gyi lha mo chen mo la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//shin tu dang ba'i sems dad pa dang 
124b5(rgyud): ldan pa'i rigs kyi bu'am/rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig gis 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la mchod pa'i yo byad bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /yangs shing rgya che ba dag gis mchod par byed par 'dod pa dang /dus 
124b6(rgyud): gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi spyod yul zab mo shes shing rtogs par 'dod pa gang yin pa des gdon mi za bar sems rtse gcig tu mdo sde'i rgyal po 'di grong khyer ram/grong rdal lam/ri'am/nags tshal gang yang rung ste/gnas de dang de dag tu sems can 
124b7(rgyud): rnams la rgya cher yang dag par bstan nas yongs su spel bar bya'o//chos nyan pa gang yin pa de yang rnam par g.yeng ba'i 'du shes spangs nas rna blags te sems rtse gcig tu gzhag par bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis lha mo de dang 'khor mang po rnams la tshigs su bcad de bka' 
125a1(rgyud): @#//stsal pa/gang zhig sangs rgyas mang po la//bsam gyis mi khyab mchod pa dang //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//spyod yul zab mo shes 'dod na//gal te gser 'od dam pa 'di//yang dag phyi bshad mthong gyur na//de yi phyogs su nyer 
125a2(rgyud): song nas//de yi drung du 'gro bar bya//mdo 'di bsam gyis mi khyab pas//yon tan mang po spyod byed cing //sdug bsngal rgya mtsho mtha' med las//sems can rnams ni thar bar byed//nga yi mdo 'di rnam bltas na//thog ma bar dang tha mar dag/shin tu 
125a3(rgyud): zab pas dpag par dka'//dpe dag gis ni zla byar med//gang gA'i bye ma sa chen rdul//rgya mtsho'i chu dang nam mkha' dang //ri brag thams cad gang gis kyang //cha shas tsam yang dper byar med//chos dbyings zab mor 'jug 'dod na//mdo 'di sngar ni 
125a4(rgyud): mnyan par bya//chos nyid mchod rten gang na bar//zab mo shin tu bde bar gnas//mchod rten 'di yi nang dag tu//shAkya thub pa nga yis ni//yid 'ong sgra dbyangs dam pa yis//mdo sde 'di bshad mthong bar 'gyur//des na grangs tshad bsam dka' ba'i//
